It is designed to be a terminal-first, extensible, and\npowerful tool for developers.\n\n## Project Overview\n\n- **Purpose:** Provide a seamless terminal interface for Gemini models,\n  supporting code understanding, generation, automation, and integration via MCP\n  (Model Context Protocol).\n- **Main Technologies:**\n  - **Runtime:** Node.js (>=20.0.0, recommended ~20.19.0 for development)\n  - **Language:** TypeScript\n  - **UI Framework:** React (using [Ink](https://github.com/vadimdemedes/ink)\n    for CLI rendering)\n  - **Testing:** Vitest\n  - **Bundling:** esbuild\n  - **Linting/Formatting:** ESLint, Prettier\n- **Architecture:** Monorepo structure using npm workspaces.\n  - `packages/cli`: User-facing terminal UI, input processing, and display\n    rendering.\n  - `packages/core`: Backend logic, Gemini API orchestration, prompt\n    construction, and tool execution.\n  - `packages/a2a-server`: Experimental Agent-to-Agent server.\n  - `packages/sdk`: Programmatic SDK for embedding Gemini CLI capabilities.\n  - `packages/devtools`: Integrated developer tools (Network/Console inspector).\n  - `packages/test-utils`: Shared test utilities and test rig.\n  - `packages/vscode-ide-companion`: VS Code extension pairing with the CLI.\n\n## Building and Running\n\n- **Install Dependencies:** `npm install`\n- **Build All:** `npm run build:all` (Builds packages, sandbox, and VS Code\n  companion)\n- **Build Packages:** `npm run build`\n- **Run in Development:** `npm run start`\n- **Run in Debug Mode:** `npm run debug` (Enables Node.js inspector)\n- **Bundle Project:** `npm run bundle`\n- **Clean Artifacts:** `npm run clean`\n\n## Testing and Quality\n\n- **Test Commands:**\n  - **Unit (All):** `npm run test`\n  - **Integration (E2E):** `npm run test:e2e`\n  - > **NOTE**: Please run the memory and perf tests locally **only if** you are\n    > implementing changes related to those test areas. Otherwise skip these\n    > tests locally and rely on CI to run them on nightly builds.\n  - **Memory (Nightly):** `npm run test:memory` (Runs memory regression tests\n    against baselines. Excluded from `preflight`, run nightly.)\n  - **Performance (Nightly):** `npm run test:perf` (Runs CPU performance\n    regression tests against baselines. Excluded from `preflight`, run nightly.)\n  - **Workspace-Specific:** `npm test -w <pkg> -- <path>` (Note: `<path>` must\n    be relative to the workspace root, e.g.,\n    `-w @google/gemini-cli-core -- src/routing/modelRouterService.test.ts`)\n- **Full Validation:** `npm run preflight` (Heaviest check; runs clean, install,\n  build, lint, type check, and tests. Recommended before submitting PRs. It is designed to be a terminal-first, extensible, and\npowerful tool for developers.\n\n## Project Overview\n\n- **Purpose:** Provide a seamless terminal interface for Gemini models,\n  supporting code understanding, generation, automation, and integration via MCP\n  (Model Context Protocol).\n- **Main Technologies:**\n  - **Runtime:** Node.js (>=20.0.0, recommended ~20.19.0 for development)\n  - **Language:** TypeScript\n  - **UI Framework:** React (using [Ink](https://github.com/vadimdemedes/ink)\n    for CLI rendering)\n  - **Testing:** Vitest\n  - **Bundling:** esbuild\n  - **Linting/Formatting:** ESLint, Prettier\n- **Architecture:** Monorepo structure using npm workspaces.\n  - `packages/cli`: User-facing terminal UI, input processing, and display\n    rendering.\n  - `packages/core`: Backend logic, Gemini API orchestration, prompt\n    construction, and tool execution.\n  - `packages/a2a-server`: Experimental Agent-to-Agent server.\n  - `packages/sdk`: Programmatic SDK for embedding Gemini CLI capabilities.\n  - `packages/devtools`: Integrated developer tools (Network/Console inspector).\n  - `packages/test-utils`: Shared test utilities and test rig.\n  - `packages/vscode-ide-companion`: VS Code extension pairing with the CLI.\n\n## Building and Running\n\n- **Install Dependencies:** `npm install`\n- **Build All:** `npm run build:all` (Builds packages, sandbox, and VS Code\n  companion)\n- **Build Packages:** `npm run build`\n- **Run in Development:** `npm run start`\n- **Run in Debug Mode:** `npm run debug` (Enables Node.js inspector)\n- **Bundle Project:** `npm run bundle`\n- **Clean Artifacts:** `npm run clean`\n\n## Testing and Quality\n\n- **Test Commands:**\n  - **Unit (All):** `npm run test`\n  - **Integration (E2E):** `npm run test:e2e`\n  - > **NOTE**: Please run the memory and perf tests locally **only if** you are\n    > implementing changes related to those test areas. Otherwise skip these\n    > tests locally and rely on CI to run them on nightly builds.\n  - **Memory (Nightly):** `npm run test:memory` (Runs memory regression tests\n    against baselines. Excluded from `preflight`, run nightly.)\n  - **Performance (Nightly):** `npm run test:perf` (Runs CPU performance\n    regression tests against baselines. Excluded from `preflight`, run nightly.)\n  - **Workspace-Specific:** `npm test -w <pkg> -- <path>` (Note: `<path>` must\n    be relative to the workspace root, e.g.,\n    `-w @google/gemini-cli-core -- src/routing/modelRouterService.test.ts`)\n- **Full Validation:** `npm run preflight` (Heaviest check; runs clean, install,\n  build, lint, type check, and tests. Recommended before submitting PRs. Due to\n  its long runtime, only run this at the very end of a code implementation task.\n  If it fails, use faster, targeted commands (e.g., `npm run test`,\n  `npm run lint`, or workspace-specific tests) to iterate on fixes before\n  re-running `preflight`. For simple, non-code changes like documentation or\n  prompting updates, skip `preflight` at the end of the task and wait for PR\n  validation.)\n- **Individual Checks:** `npm run lint` / `npm run format` / `npm run typecheck`\n\n## Development Conventions\n\n- **Contributions:** Follow the process outlined in `CONTRIBUTING.md`. Requires\n  signing the Google CLA.\n- **Pull Requests:** Keep PRs small, focused, and linked to an existing issue.\n  Always activate the `pr-creator` skill for PR generation, even when using the\n  `gh` CLI.\n- **Commit Messages:** Follow the\n  [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) standard.\n- **Imports:** Use specific imports and avoid restricted relative imports\n  between packages (enforced by ESLint).\n- **License Headers:** For all new source code files (`.ts`, `.tsx`, `.js`),\n  include the Apache-2.0 license header with the current year. (e.g.,\n  `Copyright 2026 Google LLC`). This is enforced by ESLint.\n\n## Testing Conventions\n\n- **Environment Variables:** When testing code that depends on environment\n  variables, use `vi.stubEnv('NAME', 'value')` in `beforeEach` and\n  `vi.unstubAllEnvs()` in `afterEach`. Avoid modifying `process.env` directly as\n  it can lead to test leakage and is less reliable. 2026-10-14T17:13:18.1714371Z Based on my initial analysis of the project structure and context, I have developed the following refactoring plan. Since I am in Plan Mode, I will first present this plan for review before proceeding with any implementation.
2026-10-14T17:13:18.1716271Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:18.1716821Z # Refactoring Plan: Core Logic Modularization and Separation of Concerns
2026-10-14T17:13:18.1717383Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:18.1717534Z ## Objective
2026-10-14T17:13:18.5686287Z The primary goal of this refactoring is to improve the maintainability and testability of the `packages/core` logic by strictly separating prompt construction, model interaction, and tool execution. Currently, these concerns are tightly coupled, making it difficult to extend or test them in isolation.
2026-10-14T17:13:18.5688371Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:18.5688574Z ## Key Files & Context
2026-10-14T17:13:18.6647399Z - `packages/core/src/index.ts`: The main entry point which orchestrates the flow.
2026-10-14T17:13:18.7347405Z - `packages/core/src/modelRouterService.ts`: Responsible for routing requests to different Gemini models.
2026-10-14T17:13:18.8922633Z - `packages/core/src/toolExecutor.ts`: Handles the execution of various tools (shell, file system, etc.).
2026-10-14T17:13:18.8924221Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:18.8924419Z ## Proposed Changes
2026-10-14T17:13:18.8924667Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:18.8924935Z ### 1. Abstract Prompt Construction
2026-10-14T17:13:19.0018919Z - **Goal:** Move prompt templates and logic out of the main orchestration flow.
2026-10-14T17:13:19.3242316Z - **Action:** Create a `PromptBuilder` class in `packages/core/src/prompts/` that handles the assembly of system instructions and user messages based on the current context and active skills.
2026-10-14T17:13:19.3243771Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:19.3244449Z ### 2. Formalize Tool Execution Interface
2026-10-14T17:13:19.4566307Z - **Goal:** Decouple the model's tool calls from the actual implementation of tool execution.
2026-10-14T17:13:19.6297154Z - **Action:** Define a `ToolRegistry` and `ToolExecutionEngine` to manage and execute tools. This will allow for easier mocking in tests and better support for different MCP servers.
2026-10-14T17:13:19.6298567Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:19.7327417Z ### 3. Refactor Model Router Service
2026-10-14T17:13:19.9081652Z - **Goal:** Ensure that the `ModelRouterService` only handles model selection and communication, without being aware of the specifics of tool logic.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.0236682Z - **Action:** Refactor the service to return standardized responses that the orchestration layer can then pass to the `ToolExecutionEngine`.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.0237713Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:20.0237910Z ## Implementation Strategy
2026-10-14T17:13:20.2875187Z 1. **Phase 1: Research & Mapping:** Use `grep_search` and `read_file` to map all current call sites and dependencies between these components.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.4076045Z 2. **Phase 2: Interface Definition:** Define the new TypeScript interfaces for `PromptBuilder`, `ToolRegistry`, and `ToolExecutionEngine`.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.4915874Z 3. **Phase 3: Incremental Refactoring:** Refactor one component at a time, starting with the `PromptBuilder`, ensuring that all existing tests pass after each change.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.5833427Z 4. **Phase 4: Tool Execution Overhaul:** Replace the current tool execution logic with the new engine.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.5834204Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:20.5834416Z ## Verification & Testing
2026-10-14T17:13:20.6818785Z - **Unit Tests:** Add comprehensive unit tests for the new `PromptBuilder` and `ToolExecutionEngine`.
2026-10-14T17:13:20.8146778Z - **Integration Tests:** Run `npm run test:e2e` to ensure that the CLI still functions correctly from end to end.
2026-10-14T17:13:21.0446033Z - **Regression Testing:** Run existing workspace-specific tests: `npm test -w @google/gemini-cli-core`.
2026-10-14T17:13:21.0446845Z 
2026-10-14T17:13:21.0447004Z ---
